Murph Prep 2026

2026-04-01 – 2026-05-31 | 8 weeks | 6 fully programmed training days/week

Completion Note

Every listed training day below is fully written with warm-up, timed strength/skill or workout structure, main conditioning, finisher/prehab when appropriate, intent, and scaling. No programmed day is hidden or collapsed.

CrossFit Blaze Class Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength/skill blocks are on a class clock, not loose sets.
  • Main conditioning is programmed to last at least 8 minutes for strong athletes.
  • Finishers are controlled accessory/prehab/bodybuilding work, not second metcons.
  • Scaling preserves the intended stimulus.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
MondaySquat strength + lower-body conditioningProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
TuesdayUpper strength / gymnastics + shoulder durabilityProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
WednesdayAerobic base + trunk / carriesProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
ThursdayHinge / posterior chain / powerProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
FridayMixed CrossFit intensityProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
SaturdayPartner / community capacityCommunity, simple shared work

Week 1 — Base + tissue tolerance

Theme: Base + tissue tolerance. Full 6-day training week.

Timing mix: squat/upper/aerobic/hinge/mixed/partner with varied clocks.

Monday — Squat strength + lower-body conditioning

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 air squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 walking lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2–3 empty-bar ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 6 controlled front squats + 8 alternating step-ups, rest the remainder. Keep legs durable for Murph volume.

Conditioning

12:00 AMRAP: 12 wall balls, 12/10 cal bike, 10 box step-ups, 8 sit-ups.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Tuesday — Upper strength / gymnastics + shoulder durability

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy row, 10 band pull-aparts, 10 scap push-ups, 8 ring rows, 8 DB strict press, 20 sec hollow hold, 2 ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: strict press + ring row; finish each interval with 20 sec hollow or active hang, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

12:00 quality AMRAP: 5 strict/ring rows, 7 perfect push-ups, 10/8 cal ski, 12 DB floor press. No kipping fatigue.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

9:00 upper pump: 12 DB lateral raises + 12 band pressdowns + 12 curls, controlled reps.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Wednesday — Aerobic base + trunk / carries

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 easy machine, 10 dead bugs, 10 bird dogs, 10 Cossack shifts, 30 sec plank, 1 easy practice round.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Aerobic skill block — Every 4:00 for 20:00: 3:00 zone-2 machine + 40 sec trunk/carry work, rotate stations, rest/transition remainder.

Conditioning

30:00 sustainable: 400m easy run or 2:00 machine, 20 step-ups, 100m farmer carry, 15 med-ball cleans, 30 sec plank. Conversational pace.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

8:00 trunk quality: 30 sec plank + 12 dead bugs + 50m suitcase carry, rotate smoothly.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Thursday — Hinge / posterior chain / power

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 good mornings, 10 KB deadlifts, 10 empty-bar RDLs, 5 hang muscle cleans/snatches, 2 ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: RDL tempo for 3–6 reps, crisp positions, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

11:00 AMRAP: 14 Russian KB swings, 12/10 cal bike/ski, 10 reverse lunges/leg, 8 burpees step-back.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 2:00 for 8:00: 12 barbell hip thrusts + 15 banded hamstring curls, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Friday — Mixed CrossFit intensity

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 machine, 8 inchworms, 10 med-ball deadlifts, 8 burpees step-back, 10 wall balls, 2 practice rounds of the workout movements.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Skill primer — Every 90 sec for 9:00: 3–5 technical reps of the workout barbell/gymnastics movement, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

Murph-prep mixed piece — 3 rounds for time, 18:00 cap: 200m run or 1:00 machine, 8 pull-up/ring-row reps, 12 push-ups, 16 air squats. Target 12:00–16:00; never use a vest.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

8:00 bodybuilding balance: 12 DB curls + 12 DB floor press + 15 band pull-aparts, easy quality.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Saturday — Partner / community capacity

Intent: Base + tissue tolerance. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 partner machine rotation, 10 air squats, 10 ring rows, 10 med-ball cleans, 50m carry each, review splitting strategy.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Partner prep — 8:00 movement review and loading practice; coaches assign teams and strategy before the clock starts.

Conditioning

Partner Murph-prep relay — 28:00 AMRAP, split evenly: 400m run or 2:00 machine, 30 box step-ups, 24 ring rows/pull-ups, 30 push-ups, 40 air squats. Smooth shared pacing; no vest.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

8:00 partner pump: alternate 10 DB curls + 10 triceps extensions + 50m carry.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Week 5 — Specific Murph stamina

Theme: Specific Murph stamina. Full 6-day training week.

Timing mix: squat/upper/aerobic/hinge/mixed/partner with varied clocks.

Monday — Squat strength + lower-body conditioning

Intent: Specific Murph stamina. Confident effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 air squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 walking lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2–3 empty-bar ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 6 controlled front squats + 8 alternating step-ups, rest the remainder. Keep legs durable for Murph volume.

Conditioning

14:00 AMRAP: 12 wall balls, 12/10 cal row, 10 box step-ups, 8 sit-ups.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

10:00 lower pump: 10 DB split squats/side + 15 banded hamstring curls + 20 calf raises, quality only.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Tuesday — Upper strength / gymnastics + shoulder durability

Intent: Specific Murph stamina. Confident effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy row, 10 band pull-aparts, 10 scap push-ups, 8 ring rows, 8 DB strict press, 20 sec hollow hold, 2 ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: strict press heavy 4s; finish each interval with 20 sec hollow or active hang, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

12:00 quality AMRAP: 9 strict/ring rows, 11 perfect push-ups, 10/8 cal row, 12 DB floor press. No kipping fatigue.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 10 DB rows/side + 15 band face pulls, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Wednesday — Aerobic base + trunk / carries

Intent: Specific Murph stamina. Confident effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 easy machine, 10 dead bugs, 10 bird dogs, 10 Cossack shifts, 30 sec plank, 1 easy practice round.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Aerobic skill block — Every 4:00 for 20:00: 3:00 zone-2 machine + 40 sec trunk/carry work, rotate stations, rest/transition remainder.

Conditioning

30:00 sustainable: 400m easy run or 2:00 machine, 20 step-ups, 100m farmer carry, 15 med-ball cleans, 30 sec plank. Conversational pace.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 1:00 for 8:00: odd 40 sec farmer hold, even 12 slow reverse hypers/supermans.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 18:00: power clean waves for 3–6 reps, crisp positions, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

Friday — Mixed CrossFit intensity

Intent: Specific Murph stamina. Confident effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 machine, 8 inchworms, 10 med-ball deadlifts, 8 burpees step-back, 10 wall balls, 2 practice rounds of the workout movements.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Skill primer — Every 90 sec for 9:00: 3–5 technical reps of the workout barbell/gymnastics movement, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

Murph-prep mixed piece — 5 rounds for time, 18:00 cap: 200m run or 1:00 machine, 8 pull-up/ring-row reps, 12 push-ups, 16 air squats. Target 12:00–16:00; never use a vest.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 2:00 for 8:00: 10 strict toes-to-rig/ring raises + 12 light DB rows, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.

Saturday — Partner / community capacity

Intent: Specific Murph stamina. Confident effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

3:00 partner machine rotation, 10 air squats, 10 ring rows, 10 med-ball cleans, 50m carry each, review splitting strategy.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Conditioning

Partner Murph-prep relay — 28:00 AMRAP, split evenly: 400m run or 2:00 machine, 30 box step-ups, 24 ring rows/pull-ups, 30 push-ups, 40 air squats. Smooth shared pacing; no vest.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

10:00 team mobility/pump: 12 band rows + 12 push-ups + 20 sec squat hold, quality only.

Standalone pagePDF

Post-Murph Strength Build 2026

2026-05-26 – 2026-07-19 | 8 weeks | 6 fully programmed training days/week

Completion Note

Every listed training day below is fully written with warm-up, timed strength/skill or workout structure, main conditioning, finisher/prehab when appropriate, intent, and scaling. No programmed day is hidden or collapsed.

CrossFit Blaze Class Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength/skill blocks are on a class clock, not loose sets.
  • Main conditioning is programmed to last at least 8 minutes for strong athletes.
  • Finishers are controlled accessory/prehab/bodybuilding work, not second metcons.
  • Scaling preserves the intended stimulus.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
MondaySquat strength + lower-body conditioningProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
TuesdayUpper strength / gymnastics + shoulder durabilityProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
WednesdayAerobic base + trunk / carriesProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
ThursdayHinge / posterior chain / powerProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
FridayMixed CrossFit intensityProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
SaturdayPartner / community capacityCommunity, simple shared work

Week 1 — Recover & reset

Theme: Recover & reset. Full 6-day training week.

Timing mix: squat/upper/aerobic/hinge/mixed/partner with varied clocks.

Monday — Squat strength + lower-body conditioning

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 air squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 walking lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2–3 empty-bar ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 5–6 goblet squat tempo reps, build only if speed stays clean, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

For time, 12:00 cap: 4 rounds of 14 wall balls, 12/10 cal bike, 12 walking lunges. Target 8:30–11:00.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Tuesday — Upper strength / gymnastics + shoulder durability

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy row, 10 band pull-aparts, 10 scap push-ups, 8 ring rows, 8 DB strict press, 20 sec hollow hold, 2 ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: strict press + ring row; finish each interval with 20 sec hollow or active hang, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

E2MOM x 6 / 12:00: 10 DB push press, 10 ring rows, 12/10 cal ski. Target 1:20–1:40.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

9:00 upper pump: 12 DB lateral raises + 12 band pressdowns + 12 curls, controlled reps.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Wednesday — Aerobic base + trunk / carries

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 easy machine, 10 dead bugs, 10 bird dogs, 10 Cossack shifts, 30 sec plank, 1 easy practice round.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Aerobic skill block — Every 4:00 for 20:00: 3:00 zone-2 machine + 40 sec trunk/carry work, rotate stations, rest/transition remainder.

Conditioning

34:00 zone-2 grinder: 3:00 row/bike, 20 box step-ups, 100m suitcase carry, 12 light KB deadlifts, 30 sec side plank/side.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

8:00 trunk quality: 30 sec plank + 12 dead bugs + 50m suitcase carry, rotate smoothly.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Thursday — Hinge / posterior chain / power

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 good mornings, 10 KB deadlifts, 10 empty-bar RDLs, 5 hang muscle cleans/snatches, 2 ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: RDL tempo for 3–6 reps, crisp positions, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

3 rounds for time, 13:00 cap: 18 KB swings, 18/15 cal bike/ski, 16 box step-ups, 12 deadlifts light/moderate. Target 9:00–12:00.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 2:00 for 8:00: 12 barbell hip thrusts + 15 banded hamstring curls, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Friday — Mixed CrossFit intensity

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 machine, 8 inchworms, 10 med-ball deadlifts, 8 burpees step-back, 10 wall balls, 2 practice rounds of the workout movements.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Skill primer — Every 90 sec for 9:00: 3–5 technical reps of the workout barbell/gymnastics movement, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

13:00 AMRAP: 8 power cleans, 10 burpees, 12 wall balls, 14/11 cal row.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

8:00 bodybuilding balance: 12 DB curls + 12 DB floor press + 15 band pull-aparts, easy quality.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Saturday — Partner / community capacity

Intent: Recover & reset. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 partner machine rotation, 10 air squats, 10 ring rows, 10 med-ball cleans, 50m carry each, review splitting strategy.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Partner prep — 8:00 movement review and loading practice; coaches assign teams and strategy before the clock starts.

Conditioning

Partner chipper — For time, 30:00 cap: 100/80 cal row, 80 KB swings, 70 wall balls, 60 box step-ups, 50 sit-ups, 40 burpees. Split anyhow.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

8:00 partner pump: alternate 10 DB curls + 10 triceps extensions + 50m carry.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 10 DB rows/side + 15 band face pulls, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Blaze Power & Athletic Capacity 2026

2026-07-20 – 2026-08-30 | 6 weeks | 6 fully programmed training days/week

Completion Note

Every listed training day below is fully written with warm-up, timed strength/skill or workout structure, main conditioning, finisher/prehab when appropriate, intent, and scaling. No programmed day is hidden or collapsed.

CrossFit Blaze Class Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength/skill blocks are on a class clock, not loose sets.
  • Main conditioning is programmed to last at least 8 minutes for strong athletes.
  • Finishers are controlled accessory/prehab/bodybuilding work, not second metcons.
  • Scaling preserves the intended stimulus.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
MondaySquat strength + lower-body conditioningProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
TuesdayUpper strength / gymnastics + shoulder durabilityProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
WednesdayAerobic base + trunk / carriesProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue

SaturdayPartner / community capacityCommunity, simple shared work

Week 1 — Landing mechanics + speed

Theme: Landing mechanics + speed. Full 6-day training week.

Timing mix: squat/upper/aerobic/hinge/mixed/partner with varied clocks.

Monday — Squat strength + lower-body conditioning

Intent: Landing mechanics + speed. Controlled effort that fits the weekly progression without creating back-to-back redline fatigue.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 air squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 walking lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2–3 empty-bar ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 2:30 for 15:00: 3 jump squats or speed front squats at light/moderate load + 3 broad jumps, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

E2MOM x 6 / 12:00: 14/11 cal bike, 12 DB front-rack lunges, 8 box jumps. Target 1:20–1:40.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.

Every 3:00 x 5 / 15:00: 10 power cleans light, 10 burpees over bar, 12/10 cal ski. Target 2:00–2:20.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

9:00 posterior pump: 12 DB RDLs + 12 reverse lunges + 20 sec side plank/side.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Standalone pagePDF

Blaze Engine & Barbell Mastery 2026

Aug 31, 2026 – Nov 29, 2026 | 13 Weeks | 6 programmed days/week

Member-Facing Explanation

“We rebuilt strength after Murph, then turned that strength into speed and power. This cycle turns that into real CrossFit capacity: better barbell cycling, stronger gymnastics positions, smarter pacing, and more confidence in classic workouts.”

Cycle Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength and skill work stays on the class clock.
  • Conditioning density increases gradually, but no back-to-back redline days.
  • Gymnastics volume builds from positions and strict strength before high-rep fatigue.
  • Running stays optional/limited; machines, carries, sleds, and indoor shuttles are the default until the winter weather cycle.
  • Bodybuilding finishers support joints, muscle, and member buy-in.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
MondaySquat + lower-body metconStrength maintenance and leg stamina
TuesdayUpper gymnastics + press/pullStrict strength, pulling capacity, shoulder durability
WednesdayAerobic base + trunkSustainable engine without redline
ThursdayHinge + barbell cyclingPosterior chain and clean/snatch cycling
FridayMixed CrossFit intensityClassic CrossFit feel, controlled chaos
SaturdayPartner/team capacityCommunity workout, shared work, simple movements

Week 1 — Barbell Cycling Base + Gymnastics Positions

Week of Aug 31, 2026

Phase: Phase 1 — Barbell Cycling Base + Gymnastics Positions

Weekly emphasis: barbell cycling, gymnastics capacity, repeatable mixed-modal work, and bodybuilding balance.

Monday — Squat Strength + Lower-Body Capacity

Intent: Phase 1 — Barbell Cycling Base + Gymnastics Positions. Main goal is squat strength + lower-body capacity without losing weekly balance.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy machine, 10 air squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 walking lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2 ramp sets for the squat pattern.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 18:00: 6 back squats or front squats, controlled down and fast stand, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

10:00 AMRAP: 10 wall balls, 10/8 cal bike, 10 sit-ups

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 90 sec for 6:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed volume/load, unbroken where appropriate, hold target finish window.
  • Standard: moderate load, small planned breaks, finish within the intended cap/window.
  • Modified: reduce load/reps by 20–30%, use ring rows/knee raises/step-ups as needed.
  • Foundation: technique-first movement, machine calories, carries, and controlled reps. Use bike/row/ski or indoor shuttles instead of outdoor running when heat or class flow requires it.

Tuesday — Upper Gymnastics + Press/Pull

Intent: Phase 1 — Barbell Cycling Base + Gymnastics Positions. Main goal is upper gymnastics + press/pull without losing weekly balance.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy row, 15 band pull-aparts, 10 scap pull-ups/ring rows, 10 push-ups to box, 20 sec hollow hold, 2 skill sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: strict pull-up/ring row progression 5–8 reps + 6–8 DB bench or strict press, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

E2MOM x 6: 8 pull-ups/ring rows, 10 DB push press, 10/8 cal row

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Mechanical drop set, 6:00 cap: lateral raises full/partials + band triceps

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed volume/load, unbroken where appropriate, hold target finish window.
  • Standard: moderate load, small planned breaks, finish within the intended cap/window.

  • Foundation: technique-first movement, machine calories, carries, and controlled reps. Use bike/row/ski or indoor shuttles instead of outdoor running when heat or class flow requires it.

Standalone pagePDF

Blaze Winter Engine 2026-2027

Nov 30, 2026 – Feb 28, 2027 | 13 Weeks | 6 programmed days/week

Member-Facing Explanation

“The weather is finally good enough to use the outside again. This cycle brings running back progressively while keeping strength, gymnastics, and CrossFit intensity in the program. The goal is a bigger engine without beating up knees, calves, or backs.”

Cycle Rules

  • Running returns gradually: short intervals first, then tempo pieces, then longer mixed workouts.
  • Every run has a machine option for scaling, weather, or injury.
  • Strength stays in the week, but the main adaptation is engine, pacing, and repeatability.
  • No sudden high-volume running spikes.
  • Bodybuilding/prehab finishers emphasize calves, tibialis, hamstrings, hips, trunk, and shoulders.
  • January/February can support Open-style fitness without making every Friday a test.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
MondayLower strength + short engineMaintain squat/hinge strength
TuesdayUpper/gymnastics + intervalsPull/press durability with repeatable conditioning
WednesdayRun development / aerobic baseMain running progression day
ThursdayHinge/Oly + short metconPower and posterior chain
FridayOpen-style CrossFitMixed modal intensity with skill exposure
SaturdayPartner long engineOutdoor community piece when weather is good

Week 1 — Running Reintroduction + Aerobic Base

Week of Nov 30, 2026

Phase: Phase 1 — Running Reintroduction + Aerobic Base

Weekly emphasis: progressive running exposure, engine work, strength maintenance, and calf/hip/trunk durability.

Monday — Squat Strength + Lower-Body Capacity

Intent: Phase 1 — Running Reintroduction + Aerobic Base. Main goal is squat strength + lower-body capacity without losing weekly balance.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy machine, 10 air squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 walking lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2 ramp sets for the squat pattern.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 18:00: 5 front squats or back squats, leave 2 reps in reserve, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

E2MOM x 6: 150m run or 10/8 cal bike, 10 goblet squats, 8 sit-ups

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 90 sec for 6:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit + 15 tibialis raises to support running volume.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed volume/load, unbroken where appropriate, hold target finish window.
  • Standard: moderate load, small planned breaks, finish within the intended cap/window.
  • Modified: reduce load/reps by 20–30%, use ring rows/knee raises/step-ups as needed.
  • Foundation: technique-first movement, machine calories, carries, and controlled reps. Run may be replaced by bike/row/ski calories to preserve the intended time domain.

Every 3:00 for 15:00: strict pull/ring row progression + strict press or DB floor press, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

12:00 AMRAP: 10 knee raises, 12 DB floor press, 150m run

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

7:00 pump: 12 DB curls + 12 band pressdowns + 15 face pulls

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed volume/load, unbroken where appropriate, hold target finish window.
  • Standard: moderate load, small planned breaks, finish within the intended cap/window.
  • Modified: reduce load/reps by 20–30%, use ring rows/knee raises/step-ups as needed.
  • Foundation: technique-first movement, machine calories, carries, and controlled reps. Run may be replaced by bike/row/ski calories to preserve the intended time domain.

Every 3:00 for 15:00: strict pull/ring row progression + strict press or DB floor press, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

EMOM 15: gymnastics / run-machine / press / trunk / rest

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

7:00 pump: 12 DB curls + 12 band pressdowns + 15 face pulls

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed volume/load, unbroken where appropriate, hold target finish window.
  • Standard: moderate load, small planned breaks, finish within the intended cap/window.
  • Modified: reduce load/reps by 20–30%, use ring rows/knee raises/step-ups as needed.
  • Foundation: technique-first movement, machine calories, carries, and controlled reps. Run may be replaced by bike/row/ski calories to preserve the intended time domain.

2:00 easy row, 15 band pull-aparts, 10 scap pull-ups/ring rows, 10 push-ups to box, 20 sec hollow hold, 2 skill sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: strict pull/ring row progression + strict press or DB floor press, rest the remainder.

Conditioning

E2MOM x 6: 8 pull-ups/ring rows, 100m run or 8/6 cal row, 8 DB push press

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

7:00 pump: 12 DB curls + 12 band pressdowns + 15 face pulls

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed volume/load, unbroken where appropriate, hold target finish window.
  • Standard: moderate load, small planned breaks, finish within the intended cap/window.
  • Modified: reduce load/reps by 20–30%, use ring rows/knee raises/step-ups as needed.
  • Foundation: technique-first movement, machine calories, carries, and controlled reps. Run may be replaced by bike/row/ski calories to preserve the intended time domain.

12 Days of Christmas 2026

2026-12-14 – 2026-12-25 | 2 weeks | 6 fully programmed training days/week

Completion Note

Every listed training day below is fully written with warm-up, timed strength/skill or workout structure, main conditioning, finisher/prehab when appropriate, intent, and scaling. No programmed day is hidden or collapsed.

CrossFit Blaze Class Rules

  • Every day includes a coach-led 8–10 minute warm-up.
  • Strength/skill blocks are on a class clock, not loose sets.
  • Main conditioning is programmed to last at least 8 minutes for strong athletes.
  • Finishers are controlled accessory/prehab/bodybuilding work, not second metcons.
  • Scaling preserves the intended stimulus.

Weekly Template

DayFocusIntent
MondaySquat strength + lower-body conditioningProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
TuesdayUpper strength / gymnastics + shoulder durabilityProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
WednesdayAerobic base + trunk / carriesProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
ThursdayHinge / posterior chain / powerProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
FridayMixed CrossFit intensityProgress the cycle without stacking redline fatigue
SaturdayPartner / community capacityCommunity, simple shared work

Week 1 — Holiday Community Week 1

Theme: Fully written festive training days with varied clocks, partner energy, and coach-controlled density.

Monday — 12 Days barbell primer

Intent: Festive training with real coaching structure. Keep the mood fun while preserving movement quality and the 8-minute minimum for main conditioning.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 air squats, 10 glute bridges, 10 walking lunges, 20 sec squat hold, 2–3 empty-bar ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 5 front squats + 8 box step-ups, rest remainder.

Conditioning

12:00 AMRAP: 12 wall balls, 11 sit-ups, 10/8 cal bike, 9 KB swings.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 12 goblet cyclist squats + 20 sec wall sit, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Tuesday — Sleigh engine

Intent: Festive training with real coaching structure. Keep the mood fun while preserving movement quality and the 8-minute minimum for main conditioning.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy row, 10 band pull-aparts, 10 scap push-ups, 8 ring rows, 8 DB strict press, 20 sec hollow hold, 2 ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 5 strict press + 8 ring rows, rest remainder.

Conditioning

For time, 14:00 cap: 60/48 cal row, 50 box step-ups, 40 DB snatches, 30 burpees. Target 10:00–13:00.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 10 DB rows/side + 15 band face pulls, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Wednesday — North Pole upper pump

Intent: Festive training with real coaching structure. Keep the mood fun while preserving movement quality and the 8-minute minimum for main conditioning.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 easy machine, 10 dead bugs, 10 bird dogs, 10 Cossack shifts, 30 sec plank, 1 easy practice round.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 4:00 for 20:00: 3:00 zone-2 machine + 40 sec trunk/carry, rest/transition remainder.

Conditioning

E2MOM x 6 / 12:00: 10 DB push press, 12 ring rows, 12/10 cal ski. Target 1:20–1:40.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

10:00 prehab flow: 10 bird dogs/side + 10 banded good mornings + 30 sec couch stretch/side.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Thursday — Reindeer hinge

Intent: Festive training with real coaching structure. Keep the mood fun while preserving movement quality and the 8-minute minimum for main conditioning.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 easy bike, 10 good mornings, 10 KB deadlifts, 10 empty-bar RDLs, 5 hang muscle cleans/snatches, 2 ramp sets.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 3:00 for 15:00: 5 deadlifts or 3 hang power cleans, crisp reps, rest remainder.

Conditioning

4 rounds for time, 12:00 cap: 16 KB swings, 14 goblet squats, 12/10 cal row. Target 8:30–11:00.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 2:00 for 8:00: 12 barbell hip thrusts + 15 banded hamstring curls, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Friday — Frosty mixed modal

Intent: Festive training with real coaching structure. Keep the mood fun while preserving movement quality and the 8-minute minimum for main conditioning.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

2:00 machine, 8 inchworms, 10 med-ball deadlifts, 8 burpees step-back, 10 wall balls, 2 practice rounds of the workout movements.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

Every 90 sec for 9:00: 3 technical reps of the workout movement, rest remainder.

Conditioning

15:00 AMRAP: 5 power cleans, 10 toes-to-rig/V-ups, 15 wall balls.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 2:00 for 8:00: 10 strict toes-to-rig/ring raises + 12 light DB rows, rest remainder.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.

Saturday — Partner holiday chaos

Intent: Festive training with real coaching structure. Keep the mood fun while preserving movement quality and the 8-minute minimum for main conditioning.

Coach-Led Warm-Up — 8–10:00

3:00 partner machine rotation, 10 air squats, 10 ring rows, 10 med-ball cleans, 50m carry each, review splitting strategy.

Strength / Skill — Timed Class Block

8:00 partner strategy, standards review, and practice rounds before the main clock.

Conditioning

Partner 24:00 AMRAP: 40 cal bike, 40 med-ball cleans, 40 box step-ups, 40 sit-ups; split anyhow.

Bodybuilding / Prehab Finisher

Every 2:00 for 8:00: partner A carries 50m while partner B holds plank; switch each round.

Scaling

  • Elite: prescribed loading and higher-skill option; keep transitions sharp without sacrificing positions.
  • Standard: class prescription with load that allows steady sets and the intended time domain.
  • Modified: reduce loading/reps by 20–30%, use machine substitutions, and preserve the stimulus.
  • Foundation: technique-first version with simple movements, lower reps, and coach-controlled pacing.
